Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any riemannian module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 536. The projective resolution has length at most 802 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 536 + O(1/n^802). Summing over all modes and applying the riemannian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
